### [v0.20.0](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/releases/tag/v0.20.0)

**来源**: vLLM GitHub Releases

**摘要**: <h1>vLLM v0.20.0</h1>
<h2>Highlights</h2>
<p>This release features 752 commits from 320 contributors (123 new)!</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>DeepSeek V4</strong>: Initial DeepSeek V4 support landed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40860">#40860</a>), with DSML token-leakage fix in DSV4/3.2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40806">#40806</a>), DSA + MTP IMA fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40772">#40772</a>), and a silu clamp limit on the shared expert (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40950">#40950</a>).</li>
<li><strong>CUDA 13.0 default</strong>: Default CUDA wheel on PyPI and <code>vllm/vllm-openai:v0.20.0</code> image switched to CUDA 13.0; architecture lists and build-args cleaned up (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39878">#39878</a>), and CUDA bumped to 13.0.2 to match PyTorch 2.11.0 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40669">#40669</a>). As a general rule of thumb, our CUDA version policy follows PyTorch's. We highly recommend to install vLLM with <code>uv</code> and use <code>--torch-backend=cu129</code> if you are on CUDA 12.9.</li>
<li><strong>PyTorch 2.11 upgrade</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34644">#34644</a>): vLLM ships on torch 2.11 for CUDA, and XPU is now also on torch 2.11 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37947">#37947</a>) — XPU is no longer pinned to 2.10. This is a breaking change for environment dependency.</li>
<li><strong>Python 3.14</strong>: Added to the supported Python version list (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34770">#34770</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Transformers v5</strong>: vLLM now runs on HuggingFace <code>transformers&gt;=5</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30566">#30566</a>), with vision-encoder torch.compile bypass (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30518">#30518</a>) and continued v4/v5 compat fixes including PaddleOCR-VL image processor <code>max_pixels</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38629">#38629</a>), Mistral YaRN warning (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37292">#37292</a>), and Jina ColBERT rotary inv_freq recompute (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39176">#39176</a>).</li>
<li><strong>New large models</strong>: Hunyuan v3 (Hy3) preview (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40681">#40681</a>) with HYV3 reasoning parser (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40713">#40713</a>); Granite 4.1 Vision as a built-in multimodal model (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40282">#40282</a>).</li>
<li><strong>FlashAttention 4 as default MLA prefill</strong>: FA4 re-enabled as the default MLA prefill backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38819">#38819</a>) with head-dim 512 and paged-KV support on SM90+ (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38835">#38835</a>), plus an upstream FA4 sync (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38690">#38690</a>).</li>
<li><strong>TurboQuant 2-bit KV cache</strong>: New attention backend delivering 2-bit KV cache compression with 4× capacity (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38479">#38479</a>), now with FA3/FA4 prefill support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40092">#40092</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Online quantization frontend</strong>: New end-to-end online quantization frontend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38138">#38138</a>), with docs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39736">#39736</a>); experts_int8 consolidated into the FP8 online path (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38463">#38463</a>); MXFP8 online quant moved to the new frontend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40152">#40152</a>).</li>
<li><strong>vLLM IR</strong>: Initial IR skeleton with rms_norm op (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/33825">#33825</a>), OOT-platform kernel imports (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38807">#38807</a>), gemma_rms_norm reworked on IR (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39014">#39014</a>), and IR op testing/benchmarking infra added (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40167">#40167</a>) — foundation for future kernel work.</li>
<li><strong>Model Runner V2 advances</strong>: Eagle prefill full-CUDA-graph (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37588">#37588</a>), auto-resolve cudagraph mode/sizes from attention backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/32936">#32936</a>), fused probabilistic rejection sample kernels (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38496">#38496</a>), config validation for unsupported features (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38758">#38758</a>), piecewise-fallback disabled for eagle draft decodes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39773">#39773</a>), multiple prompt-logprobs support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39937">#39937</a>), prefill warmup coverage (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40746">#40746</a>), and a fix for accuracy regression caused by stale sampled/draft tokens (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39833">#39833</a>).</li>
<li><strong>MoE refactor series</strong>: Unquantized migrated to Full Oracle Flow (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36286">#36286</a>), CT W8A8 to Oracle (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39187">#39187</a>), SharedExperts class (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35153">#35153</a>), <code>SharedFusedMoE</code> removed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35782">#35782</a>), DefaultMoERunner split (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35326">#35326</a>) and later combined back into <code>MoERunnerBase</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40560">#40560</a>), shared/fused expert output sum moved into <code>MoERunnerBase</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35949">#35949</a>), ZeroExpertFusedMoE in new framework (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35549">#35549</a>), <code>compressed_tensors_moe.py</code> split (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38960">#38960</a>), <code>GPTQMarlinMoEMethod</code> reworked with MK (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37990">#37990</a>), XPU &amp; CUTLASS MoE relocated to <code>fused_moe/experts/</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40568">#40568</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40574">#40574</a>), <code>make_expert_params_mapping</code> renamed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40671">#40671</a>), MoE LoRA refactor (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40338">#40338</a>), and MoE DP chunking removed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39107">#39107</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Performance</strong>: Optimize batch invariant with fused rms norm — 2.1% E2E latency improvement (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40413">#40413</a>); avoid <code>seq_lens_cpu</code> GPU→CPU sync (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40654">#40654</a>); cache <code>InductorPass.hash_source</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39328">#39328</a>); skip FX-graph deserialization on loading for faster warm compile (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40151">#40151</a>); CUDAGraph memory profiling enabled by default for clearer startup memory accounting (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38284">#38284</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Model Support</h3>
<ul>
<li>New architectures: DeepSeek V4 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40860">#40860</a>), Hunyuan v3 preview (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40681">#40681</a>), Granite 4.1 Vision (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40282">#40282</a>), EXAONE-4.5 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39388">#39388</a>), BharatGen Param2MoE (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38000">#38000</a>), Phi-4-reasoning-vision-15B (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38306">#38306</a>), Cheers multimodal (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38788">#38788</a>), telechat3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38510">#38510</a>), FireRedLID (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39290">#39290</a>), jina-reranker-v3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38800">#38800</a>), Jina Embeddings v5 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39575">#39575</a>), Nemotron-v3 VL Nano/Super (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39747">#39747</a>).</li>
<li>Gemma4 series: fast prefill (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38879">#38879</a>), quantized MoE (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39045">#39045</a>), Eagle3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39450">#39450</a>), block-local attention + YaRN for Gemma3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39823">#39823</a>), bidirectional vision attention for sliding layers (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40534">#40534</a>), token-repetition fix via dynamic BOS (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39842">#39842</a>), multimodal embedder norm-order fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40411">#40411</a>), plus a string of streaming/tool-call fixes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38844">#38844</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38909">#38909</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38992">#38992</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39114">#39114</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39679">#39679</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39027">#39027</a>).</li>
<li>Quantization formats: GGUF support for MiniMax-M2.1 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36965">#36965</a>), non-standard GGUF quant types with prefix such as UD-IQ1_S (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39471">#39471</a>).</li>
<li>Speculative decoding: Eagle3 for MiniMax-M2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37512">#37512</a>), Eagle3 for Gemma4 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39450">#39450</a>).</li>
<li>LoRA: Qwen3ASRForConditionalGeneration (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37247">#37247</a>), Gemma4ForConditionalGeneration (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39291">#39291</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38844">#38844</a>), DeepSeek V3.2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35077">#35077</a>), Qwen3.5 / Step3.x expert base_layer extension (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37114">#37114</a>), MoE LoRA refactor (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40338">#40338</a>), dual-CUDA-streams linear layer (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35721">#35721</a>).</li>
<li>Multimodal MRoPE refresh: mm_features-based MRoPE for Ernie-4.5 VL (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39753">#39753</a>), Keye-VL / Keye-1.5-VL (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39869">#39869</a>), PaddleOCR-VL (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39888">#39888</a>).</li>
<li>Other: Nano-Nemotron-VL static image inputs fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40724">#40724</a>); Qwen3 MoE no longer calls gate twice (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40664">#40664</a>); DeepSeek V2-Lite accuracy drop fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40673">#40673</a>); Parakeet UX / perf enhancements (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39423">#39423</a>); ColModernVBERT updated for latest HF checkpoint (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39307">#39307</a>); NemotronH default <code>mamba_ssm_cache_dtype=float32</code> with NemotronHNanoVLV2 auto-hook (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39032">#39032</a>); new TP plan styles for the Transformers backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40467">#40467</a>); GLM-5.1 fix on ROCm (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40763">#40763</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Engine Core</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Model Runner V2</strong>: Full CUDA graph for eagle prefill (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37588">#37588</a>), auto cudagraph mode/sizes based on attention backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/32936">#32936</a>), fused probabilistic rejection-sample kernels (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38496">#38496</a>), config validation (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38758">#38758</a>), eagle-draft piecewise fallback disabled (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39773">#39773</a>), multiple prompt logprobs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39937">#39937</a>), prefill warmup coverage (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40746">#40746</a>), stale sampled/draft tokens accuracy fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39833">#39833</a>).</li>
<li><strong>vLLM IR</strong>: IR skeleton + rms_norm (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/33825">#33825</a>), OOT kernel import hooks (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38807">#38807</a>), gemma_rms_norm on IR (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39014">#39014</a>), IR op testing/benchmarking infra (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40167">#40167</a>).</li>
<li><strong>torch.compile</strong>: Opaque Objects on torch 2.11 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39286">#39286</a>), AOT compile with batch-invariance mode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39201">#39201</a>), Inductor cache nested under AOT dir (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39718">#39718</a>), split FX graph via codegen (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38657">#38657</a>), Inductor pre-grad passes re-enabled for torch≥2.12 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38944">#38944</a>), strings in custom ops without compile regressions (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38123">#38123</a>), MLA + group FP8 fusion (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38877">#38877</a>), SiluMul activation+quant fusion refactor (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39684">#39684</a>), <code>donate_graph_module=True</code> for <code>standalone_compile</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39733">#39733</a>), skip FX graph deserialization on loading (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40151">#40151</a>), include Inductor &amp; functorch configs in compile-cache key (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40627">#40627</a>), respect <code>TORCH_COMPILE_DISABLE</code> at vLLM config level (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40715">#40715</a>), disable Sequence Parallelism for piecewise compilation (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38373">#38373</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Attention</strong>: FA4 as default MLA prefill (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38819">#38819</a>), head-dim 512 + paged-KV on sm90+FA4 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38835">#38835</a>), FA4 upstream sync (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38690">#38690</a>), full CUDA graph for FlexAttention (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36298">#36298</a>), FlexAttention non-causal support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40394">#40394</a>), unified 2D/3D triton_unified_attention (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40631">#40631</a>), TRTLLM minimax_allreduce_rms ported (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37045">#37045</a>), <code>concat_mla_q</code> half-types only (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37892">#37892</a>), batch-invariance-aware backend auto-selection (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40193">#40193</a>), avoid <code>seq_lens_cpu</code> GPU→CPU sync (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40654">#40654</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Helion kernels</strong>: torch.compile support for Helion kernels (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38592">#38592</a>).</li>
<li><strong>HMA / KV offload</strong>: GPU-side KV events for HMA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37688">#37688</a>), group block hashes/IDs tracked (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37109">#37109</a>), unified memory layout for offloading workers (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37206">#37206</a>), <code>shutdown()</code> on OffloadingConnector (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39182">#39182</a>), request context passed through KV offload (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39185">#39185</a>), sliding-window lookup (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36645">#36645</a>), multi-group worker transfer (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38453">#38453</a>), multi-KV-group lookup/load/store (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39401">#39401</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39402">#39402</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39403">#39403</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Features</strong>: NUMA binding for GPU workers (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38635">#38635</a>), opt-in <code>VLLM_MEDIA_CACHE</code> media URL caching (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37123">#37123</a>), safe request abort when FSM fails to advance (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38663">#38663</a>), KV connector prioritized over internal registry (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38301">#38301</a>), CUDAGraph memory profiling on by default (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38284">#38284</a>), shared-expert overlap restored (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39222">#39222</a>), <code>CONFIG_REGISTRY</code> config-class lookup fix when on-disk model_type differs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39554">#39554</a>), workspace-resize GPU memory leak fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39226">#39226</a>), SWA/chunked-local runtime admission capped to startup pool-sizing bound (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40946">#40946</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Pluggable layers</strong>: Applied to llm_head / vocab embedding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/33465">#33465</a>) and MoE layers (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/33556">#33556</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Mamba</strong>: Stochastic rounding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35753">#35753</a>), different Conv state layouts (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37416">#37416</a>), FlashInfer <code>selective_state_update</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36162">#36162</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Metrics &amp; scheduling</strong>: Labeled waiting-breakdown (capacity/deferred) metric (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38435">#38435</a>), API server handshake simplified (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39364">#39364</a>), mm-scheduler <code>get_num_embed</code> overhead reduced (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40143">#40143</a>), <code>request_id</code> on <code>FinishedRequestStats</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39710">#39710</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Executor</strong>: RayExecutorV2 introduced (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36836">#36836</a>); unified engine process monitoring with Ray backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35862">#35862</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Hardware &amp; Performance</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>NVIDIA</strong>: swapAB support for SM120 CUTLASS blockwise FP8 GEMM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38325">#38325</a>), MXFP4 W4A4 CUTLASS MoE for SM100 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37463">#37463</a>), TRTLLM GEN NVFP4 MoE with non-512-aligned hidden dims via weight padding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39510">#39510</a>), TRTLLM FP8 MoE with shuffled weights + BlockMajorK layout (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38993">#38993</a>), fused qknorm+rope kernel on SM9.0 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37376">#37376</a>), tuned fused_moe config for RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39183">#39183</a>), ViT full CUDA graph for Qwen3-VL video (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38061">#38061</a>), <code>--enable-vit-cuda-graph</code> for VLM examples (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40580">#40580</a>), default <code>max_frames_per_batch</code> auto-infer for ViT CG video (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40445">#40445</a>), fused FP8 output quantization into <code>merge_attn_states</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36518">#36518</a>), batched KV-cache swap via <code>cuMemcpyBatchAsync</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38460">#38460</a>), sm_110 (Jetson Thor) added to CUDA 13.0 build targets (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39233">#39233</a>).</li>

<li><strong>DeepSeek / MLA / Indexer</strong>: Persistent TopK scheduler for DSV3.2 DSA decode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37421">#37421</a>), DSV3.2 indexer fused weights projection (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38684">#38684</a>), Triton MLA perf fixes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/33529">#33529</a>), indexer WK upcast to BF16 for fusion (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38928">#38928</a>), MLA indexer uniform-decode optimization for MTP&gt;1 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39458">#39458</a>), DSA + MTP IMA fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40772">#40772</a>).</li>

<h3>Large Scale Serving</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>EPLB</strong>: Alternative communication for EPLB weight exchange (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/33176">#33176</a>), nixl-based EPLB communicator (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36276">#36276</a>), mapping optimization with router record for prefill (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36261">#36261</a>), <code>TransferMetadata</code> consolidation (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37341">#37341</a>), Async EPLB synchronization refactor (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37601">#37601</a>), asyncio infrastructure removed from Async EPLB (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40730">#40730</a>), replica-selection bias fix in fused_moe router (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40810">#40810</a>), Async EPLB integration test added (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40168">#40168</a>).</li>
<li><strong>WideEP</strong>: Naive all2all replaced by allgather + reducescatter (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/33728">#33728</a>).</li>
<li><strong>KV Offload / Connector</strong>: 3FS KVConnector (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37636">#37636</a>), unified memory layout for offloading workers (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37206">#37206</a>), cache_salt propagated through MP connector for per-user isolation (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39837">#39837</a>), multi-connector metrics of same type (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40010">#40010</a>), LMCache block-allocation event (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38856">#38856</a>), LMCache MP save optimization with MLA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38810">#38810</a>), <code>num_lmcache_extra_cached_token</code> in KVTransferParams (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39843">#39843</a>), offload all KV blocks during prefill in P/D (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40346">#40346</a>), DP control bundle pinned to first GPU's node on Ray (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39167">#39167</a>), FlashInfer NVLink MNNVL workspace sized to EP group (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40893">#40893</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Disaggregated / NIXL / Mamba</strong>: Full PD support for Mamba2-like models on Heterogeneous TP deployments (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37635">#37635</a>), Nixl bumped to 0.10.1 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39922">#39922</a>), <code>TpKVTopology</code> + <code>HeteroTPTransferConfig</code> unified into <code>TransferTopology</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39529">#39529</a>), NIXL EP treated as batched experts in fused_moe (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40412">#40412</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Quantization</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>New formats &amp; methods</strong>: TurboQuant 2-bit KV cache compression (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38479">#38479</a>) with FA3/FA4 prefill (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40092">#40092</a>), per-token-head INT8/FP8 KV cache quantization (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38378">#38378</a>), fused FP8/NVFP4 output quantization in MLA attention (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/35792">#35792</a>), NVFP4 dense models on MI300/MI355X and Hopper via emulation (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35733">#35733</a>), NVFP4 MoE emulation fallback for H100/MI300/MI350 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35737">#35737</a>), humming quantization kernel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34556">#34556</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Kernels</strong>: MXFP8 in Marlin GEMM/MoE with Mxfp8LinearOp refactor (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34664">#34664</a>), MXFP4 W4A4 CUTLASS MoE for SM100 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37463">#37463</a>), NVFP4 in <code>reshape_and_cache_flash</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37332">#37332</a>), batch-invariant NVFP4 linear (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39322">#39322</a>), FlashInfer CuteDSL batched-experts backend for NVFP4 MoE (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38251">#38251</a>), special <code>GptOssMxfp4MoeMethod</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39604">#39604</a>), W4A8_FP8 MoE TP&gt;1 correctness fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40310">#40310</a>), NVFP4 CUTLASS MoE OOB-read fix for non-multiple-of-4/16 expert counts (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40351">#40351</a>), RMS norm + quant fusion fix on DeepGEMM UE8M0 path for B200 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40552">#40552</a>), Gemma4 quantized MoE (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39045">#39045</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Compressed tensors</strong>: W8A8 MXFP8 linear/MoE (<code>CompressedTensorsW8A8Mxfp8</code>) (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38815">#38815</a>), CT W8A8 in Oracle structure (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39187">#39187</a>), layerwise reloading of attention/KV quantized models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38995">#38995</a>), experts_int8 consolidated with FP8 online quant (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38463">#38463</a>), MXFP8 online quant on the new frontend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40152">#40152</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Online quant</strong>: Quantized model init failure fix with prefetch offloading (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40432">#40432</a>), <code>current_platform.supports_fp8()</code> updated for TritonExperts on XPU/ROCm (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40132">#40132</a>).</li>
<li><strong>XPU / CPU / AMD</strong>: XPU MXFP4 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39857">#39857</a>), XPU MXFP8 GEMM + compressed-tensor schema (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38707">#38707</a>), XPU FP8 per-channel linear (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38316">#38316</a>), FP8 KV cache on XPU (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37731">#37731</a>), CPU W4A16 Autoround (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38192">#38192</a>), XPU W4A16 Autoround (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37986">#37986</a>), asymmetric INT8 <code>TritonInt8ScaledMMLinearKernel</code> on ROCm (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38501">#38501</a>), Quark W8A8 INT8 MoE inference (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36320">#36320</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Deprecations</strong>: Petit NVFP4 removed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/32694">#32694</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>API &amp; Frontend</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>OpenAI / Anthropic API</strong>: <code>presence_penalty</code> / <code>frequency_penalty</code> on Responses API (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38613">#38613</a>), Responses API streaming migrated to unified parser (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38755">#38755</a>), <code>tool_choice</code> / <code>tools</code> validation on Responses to match OpenAI (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40399">#40399</a>), Mistral Grammar factory (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38150">#38150</a>), multimodal support on <code>/inference/v1/generate</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38405">#38405</a>), <code>max_tokens_per_doc</code> in rerank (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38827">#38827</a>), Generative Scoring (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34539">#34539</a>), MaxSim re-enabled on GPU (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38620">#38620</a>), <code>chat_template_kwargs</code> on Anthropic <code>/v1/messages</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40125">#40125</a>), auto-detection of <code>reasoning_config</code> when only <code>reasoning_parser</code> is set (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38214">#38214</a>), reasoning parsers can access model config via <code>adjust_request</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37848">#37848</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39027">#39027</a>), effective chat-template kwargs passed to reasoning parsers (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40460">#40460</a>), reasoning parsers expose <code>reasoning_start_str</code>/<code>reasoning_end_str</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40566">#40566</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Pooling ecosystem</strong>: Pooling entrypoints overhauled across scoring (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28631">#28631</a>), pooling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39153">#39153</a>), and cleanup (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39675">#39675</a>); preprocessing/postprocessing offloaded to thread pool (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39763">#39763</a>); async scheduling disabled by default for pooling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39592">#39592</a>); <code>logit_scale</code> added to PoolerConfig (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39435">#39435</a>), then renamed <code>logit_bias</code>/<code>logit_scale</code> → <code>logit_mean</code>/<code>logit_sigma</code> for affine score calibration (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39530">#39530</a>) — breaking. <code>LLM.reward</code> deprecated; use <code>LLM.encode</code> instead (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40688">#40688</a>).</li>
<li><strong>gRPC / streaming</strong>: Streaming on token-generation endpoint (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37171">#37171</a>); gRPC periodic stats logging + servicer log forwarding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38333">#38333</a>); standard <code>grpc.health.v1</code> health check for Kubernetes-native probes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38016">#38016</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Tool / reasoning parsers</strong>: Treat <code>&lt;tool_call&gt;</code> as implicit reasoning end in Qwen3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35687">#35687</a>), <code>is_reasoning_end_streaming()</code> override for GptOssReasoningParser (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35745">#35745</a>), Mistral tool parser HF-tokenizer fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39294">#39294</a>), Mistral pre-v11 tool parser trailing-output fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40531">#40531</a>), Gemma4 streaming HTML duplication / JSON corruption / null-as-string fixes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38909">#38909</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38992">#38992</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39114">#39114</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39679">#39679</a>), HF tokenizer concurrent-borrow fix in tool parsers (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40059">#40059</a>), <code>HYV3ReasoningParser</code> no longer mutates <code>chat_template_kwargs</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40713">#40713</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Multimodal</strong>: Externally processed <code>mm_kwargs</code> with cache injection (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39502">#39502</a>), PyAV video backend for concurrent decoding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39986">#39986</a>), custom video metadata for pre-extracted frame sequences (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40133">#40133</a>), image+video mixed inputs (per prompt) for VLM examples (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40335">#40335</a>), deepstack buffer optimized for Qwen3 multimodal (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40145">#40145</a>), readonly multimodal processor warmup during renderer startup (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40797">#40797</a>), <code>mm_processor_kwargs</code> forwarded in offline <code>generate</code> APIs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40251">#40251</a>), normalize malformed dict prompts that carry token IDs in <code>prompt</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40339">#40339</a>), hotwords for FunASR (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39674">#39674</a>), bundle <code>get_generation_prompt()</code> params into <code>SpeechToTextParams</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36268">#36268</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Frontend / vLLM Omni</strong>: <code>--omni</code> delegates to vLLM Omni (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40744">#40744</a>); avoid eager import of <code>mistral_common</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40043">#40043</a>).</li>
<li><strong>LLM / CLI</strong>: Structured-output special tokens preserved in offline <code>LLM.chat</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39352">#39352</a>), <code>use_audio_in_video</code> passable at <code>vllm serve</code> for nemotron-nano-vl (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38538">#38538</a>), deferred imports save ~2s CLI startup (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40056">#40056</a>), improved MM-input-too-long error message (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39409">#39409</a>), warning when FP8 KV cache misses prefill query quant (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39752">#39752</a>), clearer DCP error message (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28443">#28443</a>), <code>--model</code> deprecation warning updated (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39518">#39518</a>), Mimo reasoning/tooling parsers mapped (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40089">#40089</a>), human-readable <code>k/K/m/M…</code> suffix in JSON CLI args (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40473">#40473</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Spec Decode</h3>
<ul>
<li>Eagle3 for MiniMax-M2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37512">#37512</a>), Eagle3 for Gemma4 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39450">#39450</a>), AITER MLA + Eagle3 on ROCm (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39616">#39616</a>).</li>
<li>TurboQuant FA3/FA4 for prefill paths (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40092">#40092</a>).</li>
<li>Mamba: default to <code>'align'</code> cache mode for Mamba-based models when speculative decoding is enabled (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40454">#40454</a>).</li>
<li>Unified Synthetic Acceptance Rate for V1 and V2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40662">#40662</a>); <code>SpecDecodeBaseProposer</code> moved out of <code>eagle.py</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40732">#40732</a>); DSA + MTP IMA fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40772">#40772</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Security</h3>
<ul>
<li>SSRF fix in batch runner <code>download_bytes_from_url</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38482">#38482</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Dependencies</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>PyTorch 2.11</strong> for CUDA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34644">#34644</a>) and XPU (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37947">#37947</a>) — XPU no longer pinned to 2.10.</li>
<li><strong>CUDA 13.0</strong> default with updated architecture lists and cleaned build-args (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39878">#39878</a>); CUDA bumped to 13.0.2 to match PyTorch 2.11.0 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40669">#40669</a>); sm_110 (Jetson Thor) added (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39233">#39233</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Python 3.14</strong> added to supported versions (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34770">#34770</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Transformers v5</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30566">#30566</a>), with vision-encoder torch.compile bypass (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30518">#30518</a>) and continued v4/v5 compat fixes.</li>
<li><strong>FlashAttention 4</strong> upstream sync (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38690">#38690</a>) and symlink-on-install behavior (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38814">#38814</a>).</li>
<li><strong>FlashInfer</strong> bumped to 0.6.8 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39959">#39959</a>).</li>
<li><strong>AITER</strong> triton BUFFER_OPS fix + version updates (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38580">#38580</a>), AITER reverted to v0.1.10.post3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39509">#39509</a>); <strong>Nixl</strong> bumped to 0.10.1 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39922">#39922</a>) and pinned per CUDA major in CI (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39851">#39851</a>); <strong>DeepGEMM</strong> integrated into the wheel via CMake (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37980">#37980</a>); <strong>fastsafetensors</strong> added to NVIDIA Dockerfile (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38950">#38950</a>); Helion bumped 0.3.2 → 0.3.3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38062">#38062</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Removed / moved</strong>: <code>resampy</code> dependency dropped (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39524">#39524</a>), <code>librosa</code> direct dependency dropped (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39079">#39079</a>), <code>pyav</code> and <code>soundfile</code> moved to common requirements (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39997">#39997</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Breaking Changes</h3>
<ol>
<li><strong>PyTorch 2.11 + CUDA 13.0(.2) default</strong> — environment dependency change, now applied to XPU as well.</li>
<li><strong>Transformers v5</strong> is the supported baseline (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30566">#30566</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Metrics rework</strong>: <code>vllm:prompt_tokens_recomputed</code> removed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38709">#38709</a>); <code>num_cached_tokens</code> / <code>num_external_computed_tokens</code> replaced with <code>PrefillStats</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37460">#37460</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Pooler config rename</strong>: <code>logit_bias</code>/<code>logit_scale</code> → <code>logit_mean</code>/<code>logit_sigma</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39530">#39530</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Async scheduling default OFF for pooling models</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39592">#39592</a>).</li>
<li><strong>CUDAGraph memory profiling now ON by default</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38284">#38284</a>) — startup memory accounting changes.</li>
<li><strong>Petit NVFP4 quantization removed</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/32694">#32694</a>); <code>LLM.reward</code> deprecated, use <code>LLM.encode</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40688">#40688</a>); <code>cprofile</code> / <code>cprofile_context</code> deprecated (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39100">#39100</a>); V0 <code>accept output buffer</code> deprecated (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39125">#39125</a>).</li>
</ol>
<h3>V0 Deprecation</h3>
<ul>
<li>Petit NVFP4 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/32694">#32694</a>), <code>accept output buffer</code> in attention (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39125">#39125</a>), <code>cprofile</code> / <code>cprofile_context</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39100">#39100</a>), <code>LLM.reward</code> offline API (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40688">#40688</a>).</li>
</ul>

### [v0.19.1](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/releases/tag/v0.19.1)

**来源**: vLLM GitHub Releases

**摘要**: <p>This is a patch release on top of <code>v0.19.0</code> with Transformers v5.5.3 upgrade and bug fixes for Gemma4:</p>
<ul>
<li>Update to transformers v5 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30566">#30566</a>)</li>
<li>[Bugfix] Fix invalid JSON in Gemma 4 streaming tool calls by stripping partial delimiters (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38992">#38992</a>)</li>
<li>[Bugfix][Frontend] Fix Gemma4 streaming HTML duplication after tool calls (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38909">#38909</a>)</li>
<li>[Bugfix] Fix Gemma4 streaming tool call corruption for split boolean/number values (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39114">#39114</a>)</li>
<li>[Tool] adjust_request to reasoning parser, and Gemma4 fixes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39027">#39027</a>)</li>
<li>[Gemma4] Support quantized MoE (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39045">#39045</a>)</li>
<li>Add Gemma4 Eagle3 support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39450">#39450</a>)</li>
<li>[Gemma4][Bugfix]: Enable Gemma4ForCasualLM to load lora adapters correctly (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38844">#38844</a>)</li>
<li>[Bugfix] Fix Gemma4 tool parser converting bare null to string "null" (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39679">#39679</a>)</li>
<li>[Model] Fix Gemma 4 token repetition by dynamic BOS injection for PT models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39842">#39842</a>)</li>
<li>fix(kimi_k25): resolve media_placeholder_token_id from tokenizer (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39344">#39344</a>)</li>
</ul>
